# Perception scout

## What this activity is

The perception layer of the routine. A scout pass walks the
game interface systematically - base screen, HUD, Events hub,
Store by section, markets, alliance panel and station, mail,
duel and radar, world map, VIP, passes, daily missions - and
records what is actually available to do as structured observed
items. It exists because the miss audit of 2026-10-31 proved
that work no static model named was never done - the scout is
how the day finds out what it contains before the day is worked.

Executions are recorded in `_records_/<date>/perception-scout/`,
one observation file per reset day, appended to by every scout
unit of that day. The full design - traversal checklist,
completeness standard, observed items schema, diff rules - is
the [perception-driven queue](../../_conventions_/local-conventions/perception-driven-queue.md)
document in the local conventions. Section 13 of the task unit
queue design fixes the unit contract.

## The job

1. Walk the surface group the unit owns - scout-base,
   scout-events, scout-store or scout-alliance - opening every
   tab and sub-page in the traversal checklist, re-grounding
   entry points fresh.
2. Record every signal as an observed item in the day
   observation file - surface, label, state, deadline and value
   where visible, the owning regular or `new`, and the red-line
   check.
3. Close no signal on silence. Every red dot or badge resolves
   to an action or to a recorded reason. Unexplained red dots
   close the unit as partial and are named in the round summary.
4. Never do the work. A scout observes only - no claim, fight,
   dispatch, donation or purchase, and no map pin tap on a radar
   stack day. The work belongs to the owning regular own unit,
   produced or raised by the diff.
5. Append, never rewrite. The observation file is append-only
   inside its day - the midday pass adds its sections after the
   post-reset pass, it does not edit them.

## Cadence

Two passes per reset day, both regulars in _tools_/regulars.json -

- `scout` - the post-reset full pass, daily-reset cadence
  anchored at the trade-day reset (02:00 UTC absolute
  boundary, about 18:00 PST now, 19:00 PDT before the
  DST end). Correction, 2026-11-03 2032: the standing
  19:00 local anchor is superseded - the 1846 live
  counters, the PR 243 duel-boundary fix and the PR 249
  daily-reset tool fix (regulars.py gated daily-reset
  regulars on a fixed 19:00 local wall time; now
  reset_utc 02:00) settle the boundary, and the tool's
  old fixed-19:00 gating is the cause of the stale
  anchor. Filed as Issue #247; this correction is the
  knowledge-page half of that issue, closure belongs
  to the daily retro.
- `scout-midday` - the midday full pass, daily-reset cadence
  anchored at 07:00 local, for state-dependent availability the
  morning cannot show.

Each pass is a homogeneous chain of scout units split by surface
group - the staged unit is the chain head, the executor extends
the chain under the queue chain rules.

## What is known from 2026-11-01

First day observation file, from the 0215 unit (ticker-0204,
unit scout-midday-2026-10-31-0700, group scout-base, status
partial). Evidence in `_records_/2026-11-01/perception-scout/`,
observation file plus queue-run file.

- Entry correction, rule R2 - the game was found open on the
  Secret Command Post Hidden Treasure screen, left there by an
  earlier unit, not on the base screen. Two taps near norm 83 to
  86,938 to 941 on the back arrow changed nothing; a third tap at
  norm 112,946, the centre of the blue arrow, returned to the
  base screen. The earlier points had clipped the overlapping
  portrait icon at the arrow left edge. The next unit re-grounds
  the back arrow at norm 112,946, not 83,938. The encountered
  Secret Command Post surface counts as encountered, not as a
  walked group.
- Base screen and HUD read live about 02:15 local - level 30,
  power 148,957,193 as displayed; HUD top row 289.2M, 477.2M,
  10.6M, 2,366 beside the cart icon; second row lightning 135
  and 11.72K with a plus button, labels for the second row not
  legible in the downscaled capture and recorded as displayed
  only, not asserted. Left column top to bottom - zombie face,
  train, truck, radio with red badge 4, magnifier, survivor
  portrait, building icon, HEROES button at the bottom. Right
  column - Halloween Check-In with a 7 badge, Events with an x2
  badge, Faction Duel, VS, Season II, Alliance with a red dot,
  Mail with a red dot, Inventory, BASE. Centre - base with
  aircraft, one building carrying a badge 5. Bottom - a NEW!
  speech bubble over a chest icon with a badge 3, unexplained
  in this unit, not opened.
- Red dot accounting - seen 7, explained 4, unexplained 3.
  Explained - radio badge 4 to the help regular, Mail dot to
  the mail regular, Events x2 to the events regular,
  Halloween Check-In 7 to the check-in surface, with help,
  mail and events units already queued for the current slots.
  Unexplained - Alliance dot, building badge 5, NEW! chest
  badge 3. Unknown items 2 - the furnace overdrive toast line
  ("Furnace total overdrive time (in sec)", furnace observed
  only, never touched, a red-line surface) and the exact
  resource labels of the second HUD row.
- Partial by design, not a failure - the unit cap of 15 minutes
  closed the group with the base screen and HUD read only.
  Unwalked and carried to the successor unit (chain
  scout-mid-2026-10-31) - scout-base remainder (building
  badges building by building, Armed Truck state, gate and
  wall signals, resource bubbles re-read) plus the
  scout-events, scout-store and scout-alliance groups in full.
  Result statistics as written - surfaces walked 1, items
  observed 12, red dots seen 7, explained 4, unexplained 3,
  unknown items 2, resources gained none, observation only.
- Cross-check inside the same pass - the Hidden Treasure read
  on entry matched the treasures unit of ticker-0136 exactly
  (M1 x13, M2 x11, M3 x0, M4 x9, M5 x0, M6 x4, M7 x13,

## Correction 2026-11-05 - the game window is windowed on an inactive Space, not in native fullscreen

- Source: the 0134 survivors record (`_records_/2026-11-05/survivor-intake/0134-queue-run.md`, ticker-0134, twenty-sixth consecutive capture failure), re-confirmed by the 0204 gifts record (`_records_/2026-11-05/alliance-gifts/0204-queue-run.md`, ticker-0204, twenty-seventh). The View menu lists "Enter Full Screen" (read from the menu-action error listing in session cc-505, repeated probe in cc-508 with the menu unchanged), so the app is NOT in native fullscreen: it is a windowed 1920x1080 window on an inactive Space, and the capture failure is the pixel stream of an off-screen window, not a broken screen-recording permission (Chrome windows observe normally in the same sessions).
- Superseded in this profile: the 0104 section's "the fullscreen game window on its inactive Space is simply not capturable" wording - the window is windowed, not fullscreen.
- Follow-up corrected: the standing "screen recording permission or capture pipeline restart" unblock is replaced - the unblock is getting the window back onto the active Space, and the owner clicking the LastWar Dock icon once is the known reliable step. Background-sent Space keys do not switch Spaces, set_window_frame is gated by the stale-observation rule, Cmd+Tab is blocked by the tool, and list_windows needs the exact app name "LastWar" ("Last War" returns 0 windows). No control route available to the background channel moves the window across Spaces without a fresh observation of the window itself.
- This correction does not change the scout state: the 2026-11-04 0906 and 0911 base-group reads (account power 150,028,269, HUD food 415.2M, iron 745.8M, coin 15.2M) stand as the last live reads. The twenty-sixth and twenty-seventh occurrences are commented onto Issue #282 by this run; Issue #284 already tracks the blocker.

Advancement measured in this unit: a centre tap at norm 500,500 advances one animation stage; the blue skip button at norm 806,938 advances one slide; the bottom-left back arrow at norm 170,914 advances slides (and loops from the chest slide back to slide 10).   battlefield +10.0K; Individual Total Points Ranking +17.5K-52.5K; Get
  Commendations when the battle settles +21.0K-45.0K; Earn Achievements in
  the battlefield +2.4K-3.5K. Achieving victory grants x1.5 for this battle.
  Rules, Rules tab: story (global warming melted the glacier, uncovering a
  hidden nuclear missile site; plan "Winter Storm" to seize the nuclear
  silo). Event Overview: 1. Hold Missile Sites or Oil Wells to earn points;
  first to 30,000 points wins. 2. If time runs out, the side with more
  points wins. 3. The central Silo opens after some time; capture it for
  lots of points. 4. Supply boxes airdrop periodically; send troops to
  capture them for points. 5. On entry, the highest-level units the
  Commander can train fill the independent Drill Ground; they do not appear
  in the Base Drill Ground and do not leave the battlefield after the
  battle. 6. In battles, units do not die; severely wounded units
  automatically recover for free. Reward Rules: unlock chests by
  accumulating Reward Points; Reward Points reset with each event. 1.
  Complete a match for guaranteed Reward Points (requires Total Individual
  Points of at least 250,000). 2. At settlement, Reward Points by total
  individual points ranking (same 250,000 gate). 3. At settlement, Reward
  Points by commendations earned (such as MVP): 3.1 at most one
  highest-value commendation per match; 3.2 players not among the 5
  displayed on the settlement screen still receive commendations normally;
  3.3 past commendations viewable in [History]. 4. Achievements earned per
  match only, progress does not carry over: 4.1 total achievements earned
  historically viewable in [History]; 4.2 progress from a previous match
  cannot be carried to the next match. Event Stages: 1. Event Start - begins
  for the first time after the server's 7th week, match battles on Saturdays;
  during off-season periods, opens each Monday. 2. Matching - Headquarters
  level 15+, several matching times on battle day. 3. Entering - once 10
  commanders confirm, all automatically enter. 4. Battle Stage -
  Preparation: safe zone, own side blue, enemy red, safe zone cannot be
  attacked. Battle: holding Oil Wells earns lots of Battlefield Points,
  Missile Sites fewer; Missile Sites attack all enemy-held buildings,
  killing stationed troops. Silo Opens: opens after some time, holding it
  earns a lot of Battlefield Points. Supply Airdrop: resource packs drop
  periodically, send troops to collect them for Battlefield Points. Rules,
  Structures tab (4 entries): Airdrop Supplies - capture to earn 1,000
  Battlefield Points. Nuclear Silo - opens sometime after the battle starts;
  capturing grants a large amount of Battlefield Points, a key structure
  for victory; gain 100 Battlefield Points per second while controlling it.
  Oil Refinery I - opens when the battle begins; capturing gains a large
  amount of Battlefield Points, the primary way to produce them; gain 30
  Battlefield Points per second while controlling it. Missile Site -
  automatically targets all enemy-held buildings, dealing damage to their
  troops; gain 10 Battlefield Points per second while controlling it. Rules,
  Reward Points tab, Ranking sub-tab (No.1-No.10): No.1 +52.5K, No.2
  +45.5K, No.3 +42.0K, No.4 +38.5K, No.5 +35.0K, No.6 +31.5K, No.7 +28.0K,
  No.8 +24.5K, No.9 +21.0K, No.10 +17.5K - all for Individual Total Points
  Ranking at that rank (matches the Rewards page range +17.5K-52.5K). Rules,
  Reward Points tab, Commendations sub-tab (8): War Hero - Total Points
  over 1,200,000, +33.0K. Elite Defender - Capture Points over 375,000,
  +30.0K. Elite Vanguard - Kill Points above 750,000, +30.0K. Elite
  Tactician - Plunder Points over 250,000, +30.0K. Grand General - Total
  Points over 600,000, +24.0K. Outpost Defender - Capture Points over
  187,500, +21.0K. Frontline Hero - Kill Points above 375,000, +21.0K.
  Swift Tactician - Plunder Points over 125,000, +21.0K. Rules, Reward
  Points tab, Achievements sub-tab (10): +2.8K - Battlefield Will Medal
  (win battles 25 times in the battlefield); Stand Ready Medal (any Troop
  helps defend the same Building for 90s); Logistics Support Medal
  (successfully collect 4 airdrops); Fire Suppression Medal (plunder 4000
  building points). +3.5K - Thunder Assault Medal (use a total of 600%
  March Speedup); Life Guardian Medal (heal a total of 16000 units); Bloody
  Valor Medal (win battles 30 times in the battlefield); Unyielding Stand
  Medal (any Troop helps defend the same Building for 120s); Airdrop Hunter
  Medal (successfully collect 6 airdrops); War Harvest Medal (plunder 5000
  building points). Rules, Other tab: Participation Conditions - Commanders
  with bases level 15 and above can participate; multiple matching time
  slots are available on the battle day. Special Rules: 1. In battle,
  commanders can relocate their Base 1 time every minute. 2. On entry, the
  highest-level units automatically fill the separate Drill Ground; they do
  not show up in the base Drill Ground and do not leave the battlefield

## What the 2026-11-09 0718 scout-discovery unit added - 99+ chat badge RESOLVED as the unread Alliance chat count, candidate rejected as a task

- Unit scout-scout-discovery-2026-11-09-001 by ticker-0704, about 07:18 to 07:29 local, status done. Evidence in `_records_/2026-11-09/99-badge-at-chat/0718-queue-run.md` (commit 5ef03f4b on records/2026-11-09). Read-only exploration of the candidate the scout diff classified after the 06:05 scout pass closed (99+ badge at chat icon, base/hud).
- Entry point (measured): base screen left edge, speech-bubble chat icon (red badge 3 at entry); the bottom chat ticker also carries the count. Tapping the ticker text itself does nothing; the chat icon opens the Chat panel. Tapping a player avatar inside the chat list opens that player's profile - read-only, closed untouched (no player details recorded).
- Mechanism (measured): the 99+ badge sits on the Alliance tab of the Chat panel and counts unread Alliance channel messages. Opening the Alliance tab clears it - verified live: badge present on entry to Chat, gone after the Alliance tab opened. World sub-tabs carry their own small red dots (Season, English, Duel); the World list showed a "470 new message(s)" banner. Private tab carries a red badge 2 (not opened in this unit).
- Rewards, cost, cycle, scoring (all measured): none - reading chat pays nothing, costs nothing (no diamonds, items or stamina), has no expiry (the count grows with unread messages and clears on read), and scores nothing.
- Verdict: candidate REJECTED as a task - the 99+ badge is an informational unread-message counter for Alliance chat, not a playable task. No execution unit should be produced from it; the owner reads alliance chat himself when he wants to. No _activities_ entry is created for a rejected candidate; the resolution lives in this fold and in the open-questions update above. The provisional filing stays in `_records_/2026-11-09/perception-scout/scout-diff-output/scout-candidates-2026-11-09.md`.
- Side observation (game state, not chat content): opening the Alliance tab showed an Alliance Announcement - season skills reset, the season has ended, all season skills are now inactive, spent points fully refunded. Noted here as a state fact; no action taken. A leader banner asks members to enable notifications for crucial Alliance news (Open / X) - not tapped, a settings change is the owner's decision, not this unit's.
- Cross-read from the 0706 events unit in the same ticker pass: the Events tab bar scrolled right shows the Wanted Boss red dot persisting after the visit, Survey Center and Community with no red dots, Community the last tab (the bar does not scroll further). Survey Center was NOT opened, so Issue #506 gets no new evidence from this pass; Age of Oil was not opened either, so Issue #477 gets none.
- Result stats: target explain the 99+ badge mechanism from live reads, achieved 1 mechanism explained and verified (badge clears on opening Alliance tab), resources_gained none, costs 11 minutes, no items spent, verified yes (same-unit live reads), blocker_category none.

- Transfer Surge (0812): the cross-warzone transfer event for the Season II window. Home: Application Day 1/4, 11.09-11.13, timer 3d 09:44:42 at first read. Warzones #2085-#2096 shown Semi-open, with Ideal Environment, Easy Alliance Changes and Talk and Connect buttons (not tapped). Your Status and Follower 0/50 at the bottom. Details: Transfer Warzone Group (32): #2087 #2102 #2110 #2086 #2089 #2104 #2115 #2085 #2114 #2095 #2088 #2091 #2097 #2116 #2112 #2105 #2094 #2106 #2098 #2111 #2092 #2108 #2113 #2100 #2099 #2101 #2096 #2103 #2093 #2107 #2090 #2109. Each listing shows an alliance ad, a language, slot counts, "Requirements met" and an Apply button. No Apply button was tapped - applying is an owner-only decision. Transfer Guide, Event Time tab: Prep Day 11/1 18:00:00 ~ 11/8 18:00:00; Application Day 11/8 18:00:00 ~ 11/12 18:00:00 (current); Transfer Day 11/12 18:00:00 ~ 11/13 18:00:00; Display Stage 11/13 18:00:00 ~ 11/15 18:00:00. Transfer Guide, Transfer Tips tab: 1 Find the right Warzone; 2 Talk and apply; 3 Wait for approval and transfer; 4 A fresh start. Event Guidelines tab read in full (carry-over rules per activity - Alliance Duel personal and old Warzone Alliance points kept; Radar Tasks active progress kept; Secret Mobile Squad ongoing tasks transfer; Ghost Ops tasks in progress instantly completed on transfer, rewards by mail; Intercity Trade ongoing trucks and trains completed right away; Zombie Siege first Alliance join triggers no 48-hour cooldown, and if the event already started when you join, you cannot join this time; Individual Arms Race points carry over if the stage has not switched; Zombie Invasion monster summoning level progress and Courage Medals carry over; Armed Truck accumulated rewards retained; see the record for the full list).
- Verdict (0812): NOT proposed as an executable regular - the only action it offers (Apply to transfer warzones) is a consequential owner-only move, leaving Warzone #2110 and the AP3x alliance. Filed as owner-decision Issue #514: whether Bears Lair should apply to transfer during Application Day, deadline 2026-11-12 18:00:00 UTC. No diamonds or money spent, nothing applied for, nothing used. The 6d base countdown keeps counting toward the Season II window end while Application Day runs.
- Survey Center RESOLVED (0825): the feedback-survey hub in the Events hub (tab bar: ...Battlefield, Desert Battlefield, Wanted Boss with a red dot, Survey Center). Its page reads "Survey Center - We'd love to hear from you!" and carries one live entry: "Season II: Polar World Experience Survey", with a blue chest icon and a button that first read Go, then Proceed after one tap, with a red dot appearing on the card. No countdown is shown on the card. INSTRUCTIONS read in full: 1 When there are available surveys, the Survey Center will appear in Special Ops. 2 Click to proceed and fill out the survey through the provided link, then return to the Survey Center to claim rewards. 3 If you exit midway, return and click "Continue" to resume. 4 Each survey can only be submitted once; a revisit shows as completed. 5 Timed surveys disappear when the countdown ends. 6 Surveys without a countdown disappear 3 hours after completion. 7 If you complete the survey but do not claim the reward before it disappears, it will be sent via mail. Mechanism: the survey itself is filled out through an external link; the reward is claimed back in the Survey Center after completion (or mailed if unclaimed before the entry disappears). The live entry shows no countdown, so under rule 6 it is untimed before completion.
- Survey Center blocker and correction (0825): the Proceed button did not respond to background input - two coordinate taps at (763,762), one click_element on the Proceed OCR control and one click_foreground attempt (rejected: no Accessibility Action ID for an OCR control) all produced no change, and the survey link never opened. The reward content for the Season II survey is therefore unmeasured. Correction, observed 08:29 in the next unit's session: the Proceed tap HAD worked, with a delay - at the next activation the frontmost app was Chrome showing "Season II: Polar World Experience Survey", i.e. the survey link opened in the owner's browser after the session ended. Nothing was filled in or submitted there; the page was left open for the owner.

No survey was submitted, no reward claimed, nothing spent. Follow-up: none for this candidate. If the owner fills the Season II survey, the reward claim in the Survey Center (or its arrival by mail) is worth one check; no unit is staged for that.
- The Age of Oil RESOLVED (0829): the leftmost tab of the Events hub (tab order: The Age of Oil, Arms Race, Winter Battlefield, Desert Battlefield, Wanted Boss, Survey Center, Community).
